Medical Visual Question Answering (MedVQA) has particularly benefited from these developments. However, despite Bangla being one of the most widely spoken languages globally, there exists no established MedVQA benchmark for it. To address this gap, we introduce BanglaMedVQA, a dataset comprising clinically validated image-question-answer pairs, along with a comprehensive evaluation of current foundation models on this resource. Consistent with prior findings that report low performance of current models on English MedVQA benchmarks, our analysis reveals that Bangla performance is substantially lower, reflecting the challenges inherent to low-resource languages. Even top-performing models such as Gemini and GPT-4.1 mini fail to accurately answer specialized diagnostic questions, indicating severe limitations in fine-grained medical reasoning. Although certain open-source models, such as Gemma-3, occasionally outperform these models in general categories, they too struggle with clinically complex questions, underscoring the urgent need for top-notch evaluation method.

</details>


### [182] [iPOE: Interpretable Prompt Optimization via Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18113)
*Jiahui Li,Sean Papay,Roman Klinger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出iPOE，一种通过解释引导的可解释提示优化策略，利用自动生成的注释指南（来自LLM或人类）来优化提示，在四个数据集上相比无指南和随机指南的基线分别提升高达31%和35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提示优化被视为离散搜索问题，但优化结果不透明，难以理解具体改动为何以及何处带来性能提升；而人类注释任务中，精心设计的注释指南能提升一致性。本文旨在结合两者，实现可解释的提示优化。

Method: 提出iPOE方法，通过自动生成（来自LLM或人类）的注释决策解释创建指南，并通过删除、添加、打乱、合并等操作优化指南集合，最终形成包含指南的提示文本。

Result: 在四个数据集上，iPOE相比无指南提示提升高达31%，相比随机选择指南提示提升高达35%。此外，LLM生成的解释可替代人类解释。

Conclusion: iPOE通过引入可解释的指南机制，使LLM注释决策和优化过程透明化，尤其在需要专业知识的领域对外行用户友好，且LLM解释可替代人工解释，降低了应用成本。

Abstract: Prompt optimization has often been framed as a discrete search problem to find high-performing and robust instructions for an LLM. However, the search result might not make it transparent why and where specific prompt changes lead to performance gains. This is in contrast to how humans are instructed for annotation tasks. Here, researchers carefully design annotation guidelines, leading to enhanced annotation consistency. Our paper aims at joining these two approaches and introduces iPOE, a novel interpretable prompt optimization strategy via explanations. We guide the prompt optimization process by automatically created guidelines from explanations of annotation decisions (either automatically generated or from humans). This set of guidelines is furthermore optimized by as series of operations, including removing, adding, shuffling, and merging. The resulting prompt includes guidelines that instruct the annotation, making the decision process of the LLM and the optimization transparent. It therefore supports also laypeople in the area of prompt optimization, particularly in challenging domains requiring expertise. In our experiments on four datasets, we find that iPOE can improves over prompts without guidelines and with random selected guidelines by up to $31\%$ and $35\%$, respectively. Moreover, LLM explanations can replace human explanations in the proposed method.

</details>


### [183] [FOL2NS: Generating Natural Sentences from First-Order Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18155)
*Mei Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了FOL2NS框架，利用神经符号方法生成一阶逻辑公式并将其转换为自然语言句子，实验表明能生成格式良好的模板和流畅语句，但随结构复杂度增加在精确语义表示和自然生成方面面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将形式语言翻译为自然语言是NLP中的基础挑战，驱动语义解析、定理验证和问答等下游应用，而现有语料库很少捕获深层嵌套结构及不同量词深度的逻辑公式。

Method: 提出FOL2NS神经符号框架，结合规则驱动模块与微调语言模型，生成合成一阶逻辑公式并将其转换为自然语言表达，能处理不同量词深度的深层嵌套结构。

Result: FOL2NS能可靠生成格式良好的模板和流畅语句，但在结构复杂度增加时，在精确语义表示和自然生成方面面临挑战。

Conclusion: FOL2NS在将一阶逻辑转换为自然语言方面取得有效成果，但需进一步改进以应对高复杂度结构的语义精度和自然度问题。

Abstract: Translating formal language into natural language is a foundational challenge in NLP, driving various downstream applications in semantic parsing, theorem validation, and question answering. In this study, we introduce First-Order Logic to Natural Sentence (FOL2NS), a neurosymbolic framework designed to generate synthetic FOL formulas and convert them into natural human expressions. It handles deeply nested structures with varying quantifier depths (QD), which are rarely captured by existing corpora. By combining rule-driven modules with fine-tuned language models, FOL2NS enhances the diversity and coverage of the generated samples. In our experiments, we systematically evaluate the framework's capabilities through both character-level analysis and overall performance metrics. Experimental results show that FOL2NS can reliably produce well-formed templates and fluent statements, but it faces challenges in achieving precise semantic representations and natural generation as structural complexity increases.

</details>


### [184] [Context Memorization for Efficient Long Context Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18226)
*Yasuyuki Okoshi,Hao Mark Chen,Guanxi Lu,Hongxiang Fan,Masato Motomura,Daichi Fujiki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种无需训练的注意力状态记忆方法，通过将前缀的预计算注意力状态外部化为轻量级查找表，解决了长前缀推理中注意力衰减和计算开销问题。在ManyICLBench和NBA基准测试上，该方法在减少内存占用和延迟的同时，提升了准确率或保持了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长前缀增强推理存在两个结构性问题：前缀对生成的影响随着序列推进而衰减，且前缀的注意力计算复杂度随其长度线性增长。已有的压缩方法仍在前缀上计算注意力，而梯度内化方法训练开销大且不便于前缀更新。

Method: 提出注意力状态记忆（attention-state memory），一种无训练的推理方法。将前缀与查询令牌之间的注意力状态预先计算并外部化为轻量级的基于查找的记忆结构，替代前缀在注意力层中的直接参与。

Result: 在ManyICLBench上使用LLaMA-3.1-8B，在1K-8K记忆预算下准确率超过上下文学习，8K长度下注意力延迟降低1.36倍；在NBA基准上，仅用20%内存占用即超越全注意力RAG性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在无需训练的前提下，有效缓解了长前缀推理中的注意力衰减与计算瓶颈，实现了准确率、延迟和内存三方面的优化，尤其适用于需要频繁更新前缀的场景。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) applications increasingly rely on long conditioning prefixes to control model behavior at inference time. While prefix-augmented inference is effective, it incurs two structural limitations: i) the prefix's influence fades as generation proceeds, and ii) attention computation over the prefix scales linearly with its length. Existing approaches either keep the prefix in attention while compressing it, or internalize it into model parameters through gradient-based training. The former still attends to the prefix at inference, while the latter is training-intensive and ill-suited to prefix updates. To address these issues, we propose attention-state memory, a training-free approach that externalizes the prefix into a lightweight, lookup-based memory of precomputed attention states between prefix and query tokens. On ManyICLBench with LLaMA-3.1-8B, our method improves accuracy over in-context learning at 1K-8K memory budgets while reducing attention latency by 1.36x at 8K, and surpasses full-attention RAG performance on NBA benchmark using only 20% of its memory footprint.

</details>


### [185] [SomaliWeb v1: A Quality-Filtered Somali Web Corpus with a Matched Tokenizer and a Public Language-Identification Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18232)
*Khalid Yusuf Dahir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对索马里语缺乏专用预训练语料库的现状，构建了经过质量过滤的 SomaliWeb v1 语料库（约3.03亿词元），并配套发布了BPE-16K分词器和首个语言识别基准，同时揭示了现有多语言数据集中存在的质量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 索马里语拥有约2500万使用者，但缺乏公开可用的专用预训练语料库及配套分词器和语言识别基准。现有索马里语文本分散在多语言数据集中，或以小规模、无文档说明的形式存在，无法满足高质量语言模型训练的需求。

Method: 从HPLT v2、CC100和索马里语维基百科三个上游来源出发，通过六阶段可复现流水线构建语料库；采用BPE-16K算法训练分词器；构建首个并列式索马里语语言识别基准。

Result: 构建了819,322篇文档（约3.03亿词元）的语料库；发现HPLT v2中存在17.3%的字节级精确重复、56.1%的文档存在可修复的mojibake乱码、10.7%的文档为近似重复；所提BPE-16K分词器在FLORES-200索马里语开发集上比GPT-4的cl100k_base减少40.2%的词元数。

Conclusion: 发布了首个高质量的索马里语专用预训练语料库及配套资源，揭示了现有多语言数据分布的严重质量问题，为低资源语言NLP研究提供了重要基础资源和可复现的方法论。

Abstract: Somali is a Cushitic language of the Horn of Africa with ~25 million speakers, yet no documented dedicated Somali pretraining corpus with a companion tokenizer and language-identification benchmark has been publicly released. Existing Somali text appears either inside multilingual distributions (HPLT v2, CC100, MADLAD-400, OSCAR, mC4) or in small, undocumented Somali-only uploads on Hugging Face. We introduce SomaliWeb v1, a quality-filtered Somali corpus of 819,322 documents (~303M tokens) built from three upstream sources (HPLT v2, CC100, Somali Wikipedia) through a six-stage reproducible pipeline. We release (i) the corpus, (ii) a matched BPE-16K tokenizer, and (iii) the first public side-by-side Somali benchmark of three production language identifiers. Our measurements reveal concrete quality defects in existing distributions: HPLT v2's "cleaned" Somali release retains 17.3% byte-exact duplicates, 56.1% of its documents contain fixable mojibake, and 10.7% of its byte-unique documents are near-duplicates at Jaccard tau=0.80. Our BPE-16K tokenizer emits 40.2% fewer tokens than GPT-4's cl100k_base on FLORES-200 Somali devtest as a tokenizer-level measurement; downstream language-model perplexity comparisons are deferred to a follow-up release.

</details>


### [186] [Multilingual jailbreaking of LLMs using low-resource languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18239)
*Dylan Marx,Marcel Dunaiski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究调查了使用低资源非洲语言（南非语、斯瓦希里语、科萨语和祖鲁语）进行多轮对话能否绕过商业LLM的安全机制。单轮翻译攻击无效，而多轮对话实现了41.8%-83.6%的有害响应率。人类红队测试比自动化方法产生更高越狱率，平均从59.8%提升至75.8%。翻译质量是决定低资源语言越狱成功的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM仍易受到越狱攻击，现有研究主要集中在英语等主流语言，对低资源语言在越狱攻击中的安全性缺乏研究，因此需要探索多语言环境下的LLM安全漏洞。

Method: 从现有数据集翻译提示词，对ChatGPT、Claude、DeepSeek、Gemini和Grok进行自动化测试和母语者人工红队攻防，评估单轮和多轮对话在四种非洲语言中的越狱效果。

Result: 单轮翻译攻击无效；多轮对话英语有害响应率52.7%-83.6%，南非语60.0%-78.2%，斯瓦希里语41.8%-70.9%。人类红队越狱率从59.8%升至75.8%，各语言提升幅度：南非语+20.0%、祖鲁语+12.7%、科萨语+12.3%、斯瓦希里语+1%。

Conclusion: LLM在多语言环境中仍存在安全漏洞，翻译质量是决定低资源语言越狱成功与否的关键因素。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attempts that circumvent safety guardrails. We investigate whether multi-turn conversations using low-resource African languages (Afrikaans, Kiswahili, isiXhosa, and isiZulu) can bypass safety mechanisms across commercial LLMs. We translated prompts from existing datasets and evaluated ChatGPT, Claude, DeepSeek, Gemini, and Grok through automated testing and human red-teaming with native speakers. Single-turn translation attacks proved ineffective, while multi-turn conversations achieved English harmful response rates from 52.7% (Claude 3.5 Haiku) to 83.6% (GPT-4o-mini), Afrikaans from 60.0% (Claude 3.5 Haiku) to 78.2% (GPT-4o-mini), and Kiswahili from 41.8% (Claude 3.5 Haiku) to 70.9% (DeepSeek). Human red-teaming increased jailbreak rates compared to automated methods. Over all evaluated languages, the average jailbreak rate increased from 59.8% to 75.8%, with improvements of +20.0% (Afrikaans), +12.7% (isiZulu), +12.3% (isiXhosa), and +1% (Kiswahili), demonstrating that poor translation quality limits jailbreak success. These findings suggest that vulnerabilities in LLMs persist in multilingual contexts and that translation quality is the critical factor determining jailbreak success in low-resource languages.

</details>


### [187] [Knowledge-to-Verification: Exploring RLVR for LLMs in Knowledge-Intensive Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18261)
*Zhonghang Yuan,Zhefan Wang,Fang Hu,Zihong Chen,Jinzhe Li,Gang Li,Jie Ying,Huanjun Kong,Songyang Zhang,Nanqing Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出K2V框架，将基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）扩展到知识密集型领域，通过自动化可验证数据合成和推理过程验证，提升LLM在知识密集型领域的推理能力，同时保持模型通用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在数学和编码等领域的推理增强上表现出潜力，但知识密集型领域因缺乏高质量可验证数据而未被有效探索；且现有RLVR仅关注最终答案的正确性，导致推理过程有缺陷、奖励信号稀疏。

Method: 提出Knowledge-to-Verification (K2V)框架，包含两个核心组件：1）自动化可验证数据合成，为知识密集型领域生成高质量可验证数据；2）推理过程验证，在强化学习训练中不仅奖励最终答案的正确性，也验证推理中间步骤。

Result: K2V显著提升了LLM在知识密集型领域的推理能力，同时未明显损害模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: 将自动化数据合成与推理验证相结合是增强LLM在知识密集型等更广泛领域能力的有效方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has demonstrated promising potential to enhance the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in domains such as mathematics and coding. However, its applications on knowledge-intensive domains have not been effectively explored due to the scarcity of high-quality verifiable data. Furthermore, current RLVR focuses solely on the correctness of final answers, leading to the limitations of flawed reasoning and sparse reward signals. In this work, we propose Knowledge-to-Verification (K2V), a framework that extends RLVR to knowledge-intensive domains through automated verifiable data synthesis, while enabling verification of the LLM's reasoning process. Extensive experiments demonstrate that K2V enhances the reasoning of LLM in knowledge-intensive domains without significantly compromising the model's general capabilities. This study also suggests that integrating automated data synthesis with reasoning verification is a promising direction to enhance model capabilities in these broader domains. Code is available at https://github.com/SeedScientist/K2V.

</details>


### [188] [Infini-News: Efficiently Queryable Access to 1.3 Billion Processed Common Crawl News Articles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18337)
*Ruggero Marino Lazzaroni,Jana Lasser,Kirill Solovev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Infini-News是一个针对CC-News完整档案（2016年至今，13.5亿篇文章）的检索工具包和索引，提供文本清洗、语言检测、地理归因和亚秒级文本模式搜索能力，大幅降低了大规模新闻语料库的研究门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模新闻语料库（如CC-News）因存储成本高、处理复杂度大而难以被广泛使用，商业档案又存在高昂费用和许可限制，亟需一种低门槛、高效率的新闻数据检索方案。

Method: 从CC-News档案中提取并清洗13.5亿篇文章的文本和结构化元数据；使用三种前沿语言分类器（GlotLID、lingua、CommonLingua）进行语言检测，并通过多源地理归因解析222个国家的来源国家（覆盖83.4%的文章）；构建Infini-gram索引（后缀数组结构），实现亚秒级任意文本模式搜索。

Result: 成功构建了覆盖2016年至今全部CC-News快照的可检索索引，支持83.4%文章的地理归因（222个国家），并实现亚秒级全文模式搜索。

Conclusion: Infini-News显著降低了纵向、跨国媒体研究的门槛，为计算社会科学和NLP研究提供了可规模化的新闻分析基础设施。

Abstract: Large-scale news corpora support a wide range of research in Computational Social Science and NLP, yet access remains constrained: commercial archives impose prohibitive costs and licensing restrictions, while open alternatives like Common Crawl's CC-News require terabyte-scale storage and computationally intensive processing. We present Infini-News, a retrieval toolkit and index for the entire CC-News archive from August 2016 to the latest available snapshot. Our contributions are threefold. First, we extract, clean the text, and parse the structured metadata of over 1.35B articles. Second, we enrich the corpus with language detection using three frontier language classifiers (GlotLID, lingua, and CommonLingua), and with multi-source geographic attribution that resolves a country of origin for 83.4% of articles across 222 countries. Third, we construct Infini-gram indexes: suffix-array structures that let researchers search the full archive for arbitrary text patterns in sub-second time. Together, these resources lower the barrier to longitudinal, cross-national media research.

</details>


### [189] [Presupposition and Reasoning in Conditionals: A Theory-Based Study of Humans and LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18352)
*Tara Azin,Yongan Yu,Raj Singh,Olessia Jouravlev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过平行行为实验，研究了人类和LLM在条件句预设投射判断上的差异，发现人类整合概率和语用线索，而LLM表现不一；最匹配人类评分的模型缺乏连贯语用推理，而推理能力强的模型反而与人类判断差异更大，表明LLM可能依赖表面模式匹配而非语用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件句中的预设投射对意义和语用理论至关重要，但在大语言模型中尚未得到充分评估。本研究旨在填补这一空白，通过平行行为实验比较人类判断和LLM预测。

Method: 构建了规范化的条件句数据集，控制前件与投射预设之间的关系；收集120名参与者和4个LLM在匹配语境条件下的可能性评分；采用LLM-as-a-Judge框架结合语言学驱动的清单评估模型推理。

Result: 人类整合概率和语用线索进行判断；LLM与人类模式的对齐程度存在差异；最匹配人类评分的模型缺乏连贯的语用推理，而推理能力更强的模型产生的人类相似判断反而更少。

Conclusion: LLM在此类任务上的表现可能源于表面模式匹配而非语用能力；强调了基于语言学理论的基准测试对于比较人类和模型的重要性。

Abstract: Presupposition projection in conditionals is central to theories of meaning and pragmatics, yet it remains largely unevaluated in large language models. We address this gap through a parallel behavioral study comparing human judgments and LLM predictions on a normed dataset of conditional sentences that controls the relation between the antecedent and the projected presupposition. We collect likelihood ratings from 120 participants and four LLMs under matched contextual conditions. Results show that humans integrate probabilistic and pragmatic cues in their judgment, whereas LLMs show variable alignment with human patterns. Using a linguistically motivated checklist within an LLM-as-a-Judge framework, we further evaluate model reasoning. We observe models that best match human ratings often lack coherent pragmatic reasoning, while models with stronger reasoning produce less human-like judgments. These findings suggest that LLMs' performance on such tasks may result from surface pattern matching rather than pragmatic competence. Our findings highlight the importance of benchmarks grounded in linguistic theory for comparing humans and models.

</details>


### [190] [SkillsVote: Lifecycle Governance of Agent Skills from Collection, Recommendation to Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18401)
*Hongyi Liu,Haoyan Yang,Tao Jiang,Bo Tang,Feiyu Xiong,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillsVote是一个面向LLM Agent技能的全生命周期治理框架，从收集、推荐到演进来管理可重用经验，通过结构化技能库和证据门控更新机制，在无需更新模型的情况下提升冻结Agent的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM Agent产生的轨迹包含可复用经验，但原始轨迹噪声大且难以管理。开放技能生态中存在冗余、质量不均、环境敏感的问题，不加区分的更新会污染未来上下文。

Method: SkillsVote框架包含：(1) 对百万级开源语料进行环境需求、质量和可验证性分析；(2) 任务合成用于可验证技能；(3) 执行前通过结构化技能库进行智能库搜索；(4) 执行后将轨迹分解为技能关联子任务，归因于技能使用、Agent探索、环境和结果信号；(5) 仅接纳成功的可重用发现进行证据门控更新。

Result: 离线演化在Terminal-Bench 2.0上使GPT-5.2提升最多7.9个百分点；在线演化在SWE-Bench Pro上提升最多2.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过受控的外部技能库，在系统控制曝光、归因和保存的条件下，可以在不更新模型的情况下改善冻结Agent的性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents leave traces that could become reusable experience, but raw trajectories are noisy and hard to govern. We treat Agent Skills as an experience schema that couples executable scripts, with non-executable guidance on procedures. Yet open skill ecosystems contain redundant, uneven, environment-sensitive artifacts, and indiscriminate updates can pollute future context. We present SkillsVote, a lifecycle-governance framework for Agent Skills from collection and recommendation to evolution. SkillsVote profiles a million-scale open-source corpus for environment requirements, quality, and verifiability, then synthesizes tasks for verifiable skills. Before execution, SkillsVote performs agentic library search over structured skill library to expose instructional skill context. After execution, it decomposes trajectories into skill-linked subtasks, attributes outcomes to skill use, agent exploration, environment, and result signals, and admits only successful reusable discoveries to evidence-gated updates. In our evaluation, offline evolution improves GPT-5.2 on Terminal-Bench 2.0 by up to 7.9 pp, while online evolution improves SWE-Bench Pro by up to 2.6 pp. Overall, governed external skill libraries can improve frozen agents without model updates when systems control exposure, credit, and preservation.

</details>


### [191] [EvoMemBench: Benchmarking Agent Memory from a Self-Evolving Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18421)
*Yuyao Wang,Zhongjian Zhang,Mo Chi,Kaichi Yu,Yuhan Li,Miao Peng,Bing Tong,Chen Zhang,Yan Zhou,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了EvoMemBench，一个从自我进化视角评估LLM智能体记忆能力的统一基准，沿记忆范围（情节内vs跨情节）和记忆内容（知识导向vs执行导向）两个维度组织。通过比较15种记忆方法和强长上下文基线，发现当前记忆系统尚未达到通用解决方案，长上下文基线仍具竞争力，记忆仅在当前上下文不足或任务困难时最有效，且没有单一记忆形式适用于所有设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体基准主要评估推理、规划和执行能力，但记忆机制作为智能体存储、更新和检索信息的关键能力缺乏系统性的评估方法。

Method: 提出EvoMemBench基准，沿记忆范围（情节内vs跨情节）和记忆内容（知识导向vs执行导向）两个轴组织，在标准化协议下比较15种代表性记忆方法与强长上下文基线。

Result: 当前记忆系统远未达到通用解决方案；长上下文基线仍具高度竞争力；记忆在上下文不足或任务困难时最有效；无单一记忆形式适用于所有设置；检索方法在知识密集型场景表现强，过程性和长时记忆方法在执行导向任务中更有效。

Conclusion: EvoMemBench为未来研究更有效的LLM智能体记忆系统提供了基准，现有方法仍有显著提升空间。

Abstract: Recent benchmarks for Large Language Model (LLM) agents mainly evaluate reasoning, planning, and execution. However, memory is also essential for agents, as it enables them to store, update, and retrieve information over time. This ability remains under-evaluated, largely because existing benchmarks do not provide a systematic way to assess memory mechanisms. In this paper, we study agent memory from a self-evolving perspective and introduce EvoMemBench, a unified benchmark organized along two axes: memory scope (in-episode vs. cross-episode) and memory content (knowledge-oriented vs. execution-oriented). We compare 15 representative memory methods with strong long-context baselines under a standardized protocol. Results show that current memory systems are still far from a general solution: long-context baselines remain highly competitive, memory helps most when the current context is insufficient or tasks are difficult, and no single memory form works consistently across all settings. Retrieval-based methods remain strong for knowledge-intensive settings, whereas procedural and long-term memory methods are more effective for execution-oriented tasks when their stored experience matches the task structure. We hope EvoMemBench facilitates future research on more effective memory systems for LLM-based agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/DSAIL-Memory/EvoMemBench.

</details>


### [192] [From BERT to T5: A Study of Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18462)
*Mei Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在命名实体识别（NER）任务上微调了两种预训练模型：编码器模型BERT（带分类头）和序列到序列模型T5（带少样本提示）。在7类标签和3类简化标签方案下，采用加权交叉熵损失训练BERT，并对T5使用两种验证策略进行微调。通过消融实验和错误分析比较两种架构在序列标注任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NER是NLP应用中的关键预处理步骤，本文旨在比较编码器模型（BERT）和序列到序列模型（T5）在NER任务上的表现差异，为实际应用场景中的模型选择提供参考。

Method: 使用两种预训练模型：1）BERT（编码器模型）添加简单分类头，采用加权交叉熵损失训练；2）T5（序列到序列模型）采用少样本提示进行微调。实验在7类标签和3类简化标签方案下进行，T5使用两种验证策略。同时进行了超参数消融研究。

Result: 通过多项性能指标比较两种架构的表现，并分析了BERT的常见错误模式及两种模型的性能差异。

Conclusion: 本报告比较了BERT和T5在NER序列标注任务上的能力，为后续实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Named entity recognition (NER) has been one of the essential preliminary steps in modern NLP applications. This report focuses on implementing the NER task on finetuning two pretrained models: (i) an encoder-only model (BERT) with a simple classification head, and (ii) a sequence-to-sequence model (T5) with few-shot prompts. Under the original 7-class tag and 3-class simplified tag schemes, BERT is applied a weighted cross-entropy for training loss, and T5 is fine-tuned with two validation strategies. It also conducted an ablation study with different hyperparameters. Moreover, the related analysis provides valuable insights into common errors in BERT and the two models' performance. Based on a bunch of performance metrics, this report aims to compare the above two architectures and explore their abilities in the sequence labelling task, laying the groundwork for further practical use cases.

</details>


### [193] [Vector RAG vs LLM-Compiled Wiki: A Preregistered Comparison on a Small Multi-Domain Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18490)
*Theodore O. Cochran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 比较了单轮向量RAG和LLM编译的Markdown Wiki两种系统在小型研究语料库上的问答表现。Wiki在跨论文连接发现上更优，但组织优势经调整后不显著；RAG在单事实查询上符合预设标准。Wiki查询token消耗远高于RAG。探索性分析显示Wiki的逐项引用支持更好，而分解式RAG变体以更低成本恢复了Wiki的跨论文综合优势，但未能复现引用支持优势。结论：研究综合并非单一能力，不同架构在不同维度上各有所长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在大模型回答小规模研究语料库问题时，单轮向量RAG与LLM编译的Markdown Wiki两种系统在不同维度上的表现差异，特别是跨论文综合、单事实查询、引用准确性和运行成本等方面。

Method: 预注册实验设计，对同一组13个问题（覆盖24篇论文）分别采用单轮向量RAG和LLM编译的Markdown Wiki系统回答，使用相同答案生成模型，由盲审LLM评判员评分。包含主实验和探索性分析（逐项引用核查、分解式RAG变体）。

Result: Wiki在跨论文综合上表现更好，但组织优势经评判员调整后不显著；RAG在单事实查询上达标。Wiki查询侧token消耗远高于RAG，未能通过更便宜的查询回收构建成本。探索分析：Wiki的逐项引用支持更好（尽管RAG整体扎根性评分更高）；分解式RAG以更低token成本恢复了Wiki的跨论文综合优势，但未恢复引用支持优势。

Conclusion: 有根基的研究综合不是一个单一能力。系统在证据组织、逐项引用支持和运行成本等维度上可以有不同的表现。本研究中没有一种架构在所有三个维度上同时最优。

Abstract: We preregistered a comparison of two ways to help an LLM answer questions over a small research corpus: a single-round Vector RAG system and an LLM-compiled markdown wiki. Both systems answered the same 13 questions over 24 papers using the same answer-generating model, and their answers were scored by blinded LLM judges.
  The wiki scored much better at connecting findings across papers, but its advantage in answer organization was not strong after judge adjustment. RAG met the preregistered test for single-fact lookup questions. The clean query-side cost result went against the expected wiki advantage: under the tested setup, the wiki used far more query tokens than RAG, so it could not recover any upfront build cost through cheaper queries.
  Two exploratory analyses changed how we interpret the result. First, claim-level citation checking favored the wiki: its cited pages more often supported the exact claims being made, even though RAG scored better on the overall groundedness rubric. Second, a decomposition-based RAG variant recovered most of the wiki's advantage on cross-paper synthesis at lower LLM-token cost, but it did not recover the wiki advantage in claim-by-claim citation support.
  The main conclusion is that grounded research synthesis is not a single capability. Systems can differ in how well they organize evidence, how well their citations support each claim, and how much they cost to run. In this study, no architecture was best on all three.

</details>


### [194] [Implicit Hierarchical GRPO: Decoupling Tool Invocation from Execution for Tool-Integrated Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18500)
*Li Wang,Xiaohan Wang,Xiaodong Lu,Zipeng Zhang,Jinyang Wu,Jiajun Chai,Wei Lin,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出延迟执行机制解耦工具调用与执行，通过分层控制框架和隐式分层策略（IH-GRPO算法）提升LLM的推理性能，在多个数学推理基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将工具调用与立即执行紧密耦合，这种即时工具交互会打断LLM的推理连贯性并限制其表达能力，从而降低推理性能。

Method: 首次形式化提出将工具调用与执行解耦的问题，引入带显式控制的延迟执行机制来增强工具集成推理（TIR）；提出分层控制框架，理论上推导出替代损失函数，使隐式分层策略学习等价于显式分层策略的行为，进而提出IH-GRPO算法。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B上，针对六个域外数学推理基准，相较最强基线方法分别获得1.87%、2.16%、2.53%的绝对提升，并在其他领域也取得一致性能增益。

Conclusion: 解耦工具调用与执行、采用延迟执行策略能够有效提升LLM在工具集成推理任务中的表现，IH-GRPO算法具有跨模型的泛化性和跨领域的适应性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have increasingly leveraged tool invocation to enhance their reasoning capabilities. However, existing approaches typically tightly couple tool invocation with immediate execution. Such immediate tool interaction may disrupt the reasoning coherence of LLMs and constrain their expressivity, ultimately degrading reasoning performance. To this end, for the first time, we propose and formalize the problem of decoupling tool invocation from execution during reasoning, and introduce delayed execution with explicit control to enhance tool-integrated reasoning (TIR). Furthermore, we propose a hierarchical control framework and theoretically derive a surrogate loss that enables an implicitly hierarchical policy to learn behavior equivalent to that of an explicit hierarchical policy, leading to the proposed IH-GRPO algorithm. Extensive experiments on IH-GRPO achieve absolute improvements of 1.87\%, 2.16\%, and 2.53\% on Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B, and Qwen3-8B across six out-of-domain mathematical reasoning benchmarks over the strongest baseline method, while also yielding consistent performance gains in other domains. Our code is available at https://github.com/Lumina04/IH-GRPO-01.

</details>


### [195] [Easier to Judge than to Find: Predicting In-Context Learning Success for Demonstration Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18512)
*Haochun Wang,Chaofen Yang,Jiatong Liu,Jingbo Wang,Zewen Qiang,Sendong Zhao,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiSP框架，基于“演示选择易于判断而非搜寻”的理念，通过分层采样和轻量级路由器预测查询难度，并训练等级特定的判断器，在ICL中实现了最高平均准确率和高达23倍的端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在上下文学习中，演示（demonstration）的选择对性能高度敏感，但搜索最优演示组合的计算成本极大。作者认为判断一个查询-演示对是否有效比直接搜索最优解更廉价、更通用。

Method: DiSP包含三阶段：1) 随机演示试验估算每个训练查询的成功率；2) 训练轻量级路由器根据查询预测难度等级；3) 为每个难度等级训练专用的判断器。推理时采用带显式预算的“接受即停止”策略，未找到合适上下文时发出诊断风险标签。

Result: 在Llama-3-8B和Qwen-2.5-7B上的五个分类数据集中，DiSP取得了最佳平均准确率，比强学习基线提升最高3.4%，同时实现高达23倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: 验证了“演示选择易于判断而非搜寻”这一核心假设的有效性，为ICL中高效且可扩展的演示选择提供了新范式。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) is highly sensitive to which demonstrations appear in the prompt, but selecting them is expensive because the space of possible demonstration contexts and combinations is enormous. We argue that demonstration selection is \emph{easier to judge than to find}: predicting whether a specific query--context pair $(q,D)$ will succeed is cheaper and more general than searching for an optimal $D^\star$. Based on this insight, we propose DiSP, a sample-and-judge framework that stratifies queries by difficulty. DiSP runs random demonstration trials to estimate success rate of each training query, trains a lightweight router to predict difficulty from the query, and trains level-specific judges for sampled demonstrations. At inference, DiSP performs stop-on-acceptance judging under an explicit budget, emitting diagnostic risk tags when no suitable context is found. Across five classification datasets with Llama~3--8B and Qwen~2.5--7B, DiSP achieves the best average accuracy, improving over strong learned selection baselines by up to 3.4\%, while achieving up to $23\times$ end-to-end wall-clock speedup.

</details>


### [196] [STT-Arena: A More Realistic Environment for Tool-Using with Spatio-Temporal Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18548)
*Tingfeng Hui,Hao Xu,Pengyu Zhu,Hongsheng Xin,Kun Zhan,Sen Su,Chunxiao Liu,Ning Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出STT-Arena基准，评估LLM在时空动态环境中的自适应重规划能力。发现前沿模型（包括Claude-4.6-Opus）准确率不足40%，识别三种失败模式，并通过迭代轨迹精炼+在线RL训练出4B参数模型超越前沿LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态基准主要关注LLM检测时间变化的能力，忽略了在时空动态下进行自适应重规划的挑战，而实际部署的智能体应用必须能应对任务中断并调整规划。

Method: 构建包含227个交互任务、9种时空冲突类型、4种可解性级别的STT-Arena基准；基于失败轨迹分析识别三种错误模式；提出迭代轨迹精炼技术消除训练数据中的失败模式，结合在线强化学习训练STT-Agent-4B。

Result: 前沿模型（包括Claude-4.6-Opus）在STT-Arena上准确率低于40%；STT-Agent-4B（4B参数）超越所有前沿LLM。

Conclusion: 时空动态推理对现有LLM是根本性挑战；通过消除失败模式的训练数据精炼+在线RL，小模型可在此任务上超越大模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed in real-world agentic applications must be capable of replanning and adapting when mid-task disruptions invalidate their prior decisions. Existing dynamic benchmarks primarily measure whether LLMs can detect temporal changes in a timely manner, leaving the complementary challenge of adaptive replanning under spatio-temporal dynamics largely unexplored. We introduce STT-Arena (Spatio-Temporal Tool-Use Arena), a benchmark of 227 high-quality interactive tasks spanning nine spatio-temporal conflict types and four solvability levels. Each task is grounded in a realistic, executable environment equipped with injected spatio-temporal triggers that can abruptly invalidate an ongoing plan, forcing the model to detect the state shift and construct a revised execution strategy. Extensive evaluation of frontier LLMs reveals that even the SOTA proprietary models, including Claude-4.6-Opus, achieves less than 40\% overall accuracies, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of spatio-temporal dynamic reasoning. Systematic analysis of failure trajectories uncovers three recurring error modes of existing models: Stale-State Execution, Misdiagnosis of Dynamic Triggers, and Missing Post-Adaptation Verification. Guided by these findings, we propose an iterative trajectory refinement technique that eliminates these failure patterns from training data, and combine it with online RL to produce STT-Agent-4B which outperforms frontier LLMs on STT-Arena.

</details>


### [197] [Monitoring the Internal Monologue: Probe Trajectories Reveal Reasoning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18549)
*Maciej Chrabąszcz,Aleksander Szymczyk,Marcin Sendera,Tomasz Trzciński,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过探针轨迹（probe trajectories）监控大型推理模型（LRM）的行为，利用链式思维（CoT）推理过程中的隐藏表示预测未来行为，发现全轨迹分析优于单点预测，并提取信号处理特征（波动性、趋势、稳态）显著提升分离效果。模板训练数据与动态生成数据表现接近，最大池化达95% AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LRM的CoT推理为安全监控提供了新机会，但CoT并不总是忠实于最终输出，降低了其作为监控工具的可靠性。因此，需要探索是否可以从提示和CoT的隐藏表示中预测模型未来行为。

Method: 在每个生成token处评估探针，构建探针轨迹（概念概率的连续演化）；提取信号处理特征（波动性、趋势、稳态行为）表征时间动态；比较不同池化操作（平均池化、最后token、最大池化）；使用模板训练数据与动态生成数据对比。

Result: 全轨迹分析比单点预测更好地区分未来模型行为；信号处理特征显著提升未来模型状态的分离效果；模板训练数据与动态生成数据表现接近；最大池化达95% AUROC，而平均池化和最后token方法接近随机水平。在四个数据集和四个推理模型上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 探针轨迹可作为监控LRM行为的互补框架，轨迹特征编码了任务特定动态，能够改善结果可分离性。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) introduce new opportunities for safety monitoring through their Chain of Thought (CoT) reasoning. However, CoT is not always faithful to the model's final output, undermining its reliability as a monitoring tool. To address this, we investigate the hidden representations of LRMs to determine whether future behavior can be predicted from prompt and CoT representations. By evaluating a probe at each generated token, we construct a probe trajectory, the continuous evolution of a concept's probability across the reasoning process. We find that future model behavior is more distinguishable when examined over the full trajectory than from a single static prediction. To characterize these temporal dynamics, we extract signal-processing features that capture volatility, trend, and steady-state behavior, significantly improving the separation of future model states. We also present two methodological insights. First, template-based training data achieves near-parity with dynamically generated model responses, eliminating the need for a costly initial inference and labeling. Second, the choice of pooling operation is critical: average-pooling and last-token methods collapse to near-random performance, while max-pooling achieves up to 95% AUROC and yields stable probe trajectories. Using four datasets and four reasoning models across the domains of safety and mathematics, we demonstrate that trajectory features encode task-specific dynamics that improve outcome separability. These findings establish probe trajectories as a complementary framework for monitoring LRM behavior.
  Warning: This article contains potentially harmful content.

</details>


### [198] [Readers make targeted regressions to plausible errors in reanalysis of "noisy-channel garden-path" sentences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18563)
*Thomas Hikaru Clark,Roger Levy,Edward Gibson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过眼动实验发现，读者在处理“噪声通道花园小径句”时，会表现出针对性的回视（眼动回溯到可能出错区域），这一模式与含重分析的噪声通道处理模型的后验推断一致，支持了阅读动态中基于推理的错误检测机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究读者在理解语言输入时，如何逐步推断其含义，特别是面对看似合法但后期出现违背预期的“噪声通道花园小径句”时，读者如何进行推断和错误检测。

Method: 通过眼动实验研究读者对噪声通道花园小径句的阅读动态，分析回视模式，并与含重分析的噪声通道处理模型的后验推断进行比较。

Result: 发现读者表现出针对性的回视，即目光移向后期信息提示下可能出错的位置，该模式与噪声通道处理模型的理论预测一致。

Conclusion: 阅读动态中出现的针对性回视支持了噪声通道语言理解理论，并为阅读动态的信息论解释提供了证据。

Abstract: A key question in psycholinguistics is how inferences about the meaning of linguistic input unfold incrementally a comprehender's mind. In this work, we study reading dynamics for ``noisy-channel garden-path'' sentences, which temporarily appear well-formed but feature late-appearing violations of expectation that can be resolved not by inferring an alternative syntactic structure, but by inferring the presence of an error. We find evidence for targeted regressions -- eye movements towards regions that are promising loci of possible errors in light of later-arriving information, showing patterns consistent with the posterior inferences of a model of noisy-channel processing with reanalysis. We discuss the implications of these findings for theories of noisy-channel language comprehension and information-theoretic explanations of reading dynamics.

</details>


### [199] [LongMINT: Evaluating Memory under Multi-Target Interference in Long-Horizon Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18565)
*Hyunji Lee,Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Joykirat Singh,Zaid Khan,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Mohit Bansal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LongMINT基准，用于评估记忆增强型智能体在长程、高干扰、信息频繁更新的现实场景下的表现。基准涵盖15.6k问答对，平均上下文长度138.8k tokens，包含单目标召回和多目标聚合两类问题。对7种代表性系统的评估显示，平均准确率仅27.9%，尤其在多证据聚合推理任务上表现更差，主要瓶颈在于检索和记忆构建环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准专注于静态、独立的召回任务，无法捕捉现实世界中信息频繁更新、记忆间相互干扰的动态交互场景。为了填补这一空白，作者研究当前记忆增强型智能体在真实、高干扰、长程设置下的表现。

Method: 构建LongMINT基准，包含(1)长程、高度互联且频繁更新的上下文；(2)四个多样领域：状态追踪、多轮对话、维基百科修订、GitHub提交；(3)两类问题：单目标召回和多目标聚合。评估7种代表性系统，包括长上下文LLM、RAG和记忆增强智能体框架。

Result: 所有系统平均准确率仅27.9%，多目标聚合任务表现更差。性能主要受限于检索和记忆构建。当前记忆系统在回忆被后续上下文修订或干扰的早期事实时表现不佳，性能随中间更新次数增加而下降。

Conclusion: LongMINT揭示了现有记忆增强系统在长程、高干扰场景下的严重不足，尤其在多证据聚合推理方面。检索和记忆构建是主要瓶颈，且系统对信息更新的鲁棒性差，提示该方向仍需重大改进。

Abstract: Real-world agents operate over long and evolving horizons, where information is repeatedly updated and may interfere across memories, requiring accurate recall and aggregated reasoning over multiple pieces of information. However, existing benchmarks focus on static, independent recall and fail to capture these dynamic interactions between evolving memories. In this paper, we study how current memory-augmented agents perform in realistic, interference-heavy, long-horizon settings across diverse domains and question types. We introduce LongMINT (Long-Horizon Memory under INTerference), a benchmark featuring (1) long, highly interconnected contexts with frequently updated information that induces substantial interference, (2) diverse domains (state tracking, multi-turn dialogue, Wikipedia revisions, and GitHub commits), enabling evaluation of domain generalization, and (3) diverse question types that assess robustness to interference, including (i) single-target recall tasks requiring retrieval of a specific target from long contexts, and (ii) multi-target aggregation tasks requiring reasoning over multiple relevant pieces of information. Overall, LongMINT has 15.6k question-answering pairs over long-horizon contexts averaging 138.8k tokens and extending up to 1.8M tokens per instance. We evaluate 7 representative systems, including vanilla long-context LLMs, RAG, and memory-augmented agent frameworks. Across all systems, we observe consistently low performance (avg. 27.9% accuracy), especially on questions requiring aggregated reasoning over multiple pieces of evidence. Our analysis shows that performance is primarily limited by retrieval and memory construction. Furthermore, current memory systems struggle to recall and reason over earlier facts that are later revised or interfered with by subsequent context, with performance degrading as the number of intervening updates increases.

</details>


### [200] [GUT-IS: A Data-Driven Approach to Integrating Constructs and Their Relations in Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18567)
*Maximilian Reinhardt,Jonas Scharfenberger,Burkhardt Funk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合任务自适应文本嵌入与聚类的方法，用于将结构方程模型整合为统一模型，通过显式权衡语义纯度与简约性来选择最优的构念分组方案，并在两个IS领域数据集上进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构方程模型在IS研究中被广泛使用，但构念定义不一致严重阻碍了知识的累积性发展，亟需一种系统化的整合方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 采用任务自适应的文本嵌入与聚类技术生成候选构念分组集，然后通过一个显式权衡语义纯度与聚类数量简约性的损失函数选择最优解。

Result: 在两个IS领域数据集上对提出的方法论进行了实证评估与探索，展示了该方法在平衡语义纯度与简约性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式地权衡纯度与简约性，该方法能够分析当优先级从纯度转向简约性时构念分组及其关系如何变化，为结构方程模型的统一整合提供了系统化的解决路径。

Abstract: Structural equation modeling is widely used in IS research. However, inconsistent construct definitions impede the cumulative development of knowledge. In this work, we present an approach that aims at the integration of structural equation models into a unified model: We use a combination of task-adapted text embeddings and clustering to produce a candidate set of construct groupings. Subsequently, we select the optimal solution using a loss function that explicitly trades off semantic purity and parsimony in the number of clusters. By making this trade-off explicit, our approach allows to analyze how construct groupings and their relations change as one shifts the priority from purity to parsimony. Empirically, we evaluate and explore the proposed methodology on two datasets from the IS domain.

</details>


### [201] [MA$^{2}$P: A Meta-Cognitive Autonomous Intelligent Agents Framework for Complex Persuasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18572)
*Dingyi Zhang,Ziqing Zhuang,Linhai Zhang,Ziyang Gao,Deyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MA$^{2}$P元认知自主智能体框架，通过多智能体架构和元认知配置器解决复杂说服对话中的生成质量差和跨域性能波动问题，实验证明该方法在说服成功率上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前说服对话生成方法在面对被说服者内隐状态时产生通用或弱相关的回复，且大语言模型在不同领域间因知识覆盖不均和推理泛化受限而表现差异显著。

Method: 提出MA$^{2}$P框架：(1) 自主多智能体架构，协调感知管理、心理状态推理、策略执行、记忆维护和性能评估；(2) 元认知配置器，在初始阶段从结构化知识库中选择合适的元策略以引导后续推理和规划。

Result: 实验结果表明该方法在说服成功率上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MA$^{2}$P框架通过元认知机制和多智能体协同，有效提升了复杂场景下的说服对话生成效果。

Abstract: Persuasive dialogue generation plays a vital role in decision-making, negotiation, counseling, and behavior change, yet it remains a challenging problem. In complex persuasion where the persuadee's internal states are not expressed clearly, the persuader must interpret responses, infer the persuadee's latent mental states (e.g., beliefs and desires), and translate them into targeted, strategy-consistent actions; however, current approaches often produce generic or weakly grounded responses even when such cues are identified. Moreover, although large language models (LLMs) can generate persuasive content, their performance varies substantially across domains due to uneven knowledge coverage and limited reasoning generalization. To address these challenges, we propose MA$^{2}$P, a meta-cognitive autonomous intelligent agent framework for complex persuasion. Specifically, we develop an autonomous multi-agent architecture that coordinates perception management, mental-state inference, strategy execution, memory maintenance, and performance evaluation. To mitigate cross-domain performance variation, we further design a meta-cognitive configurator that selects an appropriate meta-strategy from a structured knowledge base at the outset, thereby guiding subsequent reasoning and planning. Experimental results show that our approach achieves a higher persuasion success rate than baselines.

</details>


### [202] [Language-Switching Triggers Take a Latent Detour Through Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18646)
*Francis Kulumba,Wissam Antoun,Théo Lasnier,Benoît Sagot,Djamé Seddah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过逆向工程方法，识别并解析了8B参数自回归语言模型中语言切换后门攻击的内部电路机制，发现该攻击通过三阶段流程（早期注意力头组合触发令牌→中层正交子空间传播→末层MLP转换为法语logits）实现，且整个电路流经单一位置的串行瓶颈，揭示了正交潜在编码使现有防御机制失效的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型的后门攻击日益成为安全问题，但触发序列如何劫持模型计算的内部机制尚不明确，缺乏对后门电路原理的深入理解。

Method: 通过逆向工程方法，对8B参数自回归语言模型中语言切换后门（三词拉丁触发令牌将英语输出重定向为法语）进行电路分解，识别出三阶段处理流程：早期层的分布式注意力头将触发令牌组合到最后一个序列位置；中层信号在正交于模型自然语言身份方向的子空间中传播；末层MLP将潜在信号转换为法语logits。并验证了串行瓶颈位置的关键作用。

Result: 发现了后门攻击的三阶段电路机制；识别出单一位置的串行瓶颈（破坏该位置可消除触发效果但损害模型能力）；揭示了后门利用正交于语言身份方向的潜在编码，使现有基于中间表示的语言信号防御机制失效。

Conclusion: 后门攻击可利用正交潜在编码绕过检测，需开发新的防御策略；对模型内部机制的深入理解有助于设计更鲁棒的安全防护。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks on language models pose a growing security concern, yet the internal mechanisms by which a trigger sequence hijacks model computations remain poorly understood. We identify a circuit underlying a language-switching backdoor in an 8B-parameter autoregressive language model, where a three-word Latin trigger (nine tokens) redirects English output to French. We decompose the circuit into three phases: (1) distributed attention heads at early layers compose the trigger tokens into the last sequence position; (2) the resulting signal propagates through mid-layers in a subspace orthogonal to the model's natural language-identity direction; (3) the MLP at the final layer converts this latent signal into French logits. The entire circuit flows through a serial bottleneck at a single position: corrupting that position at any layer entirely mitigate the trigger but also hinder the model's capabilities. The orthogonal latent encoding suggests that defenses that search for language-like signals in intermediate representations would miss this trigger entirely.

</details>


### [203] [EnvFactory: Scaling Tool-Use Agents via Executable Environments Synthesis and Robust RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18703)
*Minrui Xu,Zilin Wang,Mengyi DENG,Zhiwei Li,Zhicheng Yang,Xiao Zhu,Yinhong Liu,Boyu Zhu,Baiyu Huang,Chao Chen,Heyuan Deng,Fei Mi,Lifeng Shang,Xingshan Zeng,Zhijiang Guo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EnvFactory是一个全自动框架，通过从真实资源自主探索和验证状态化、可执行的工具环境，并利用拓扑感知采样和校准细化合成自然的多轮轨迹，解决了Agentic RL中缺乏可扩展执行环境和真实训练数据的两大瓶颈。仅用85个验证环境和2575条轨迹，在多个基准测试上显著提升了Qwen3系列模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM工具使用能力的Agentic RL方法面临两大挑战：缺乏可扩展、鲁棒的执行环境，以及缺乏捕捉隐式人类推理的真实训练数据。现有方法依赖昂贵的真实API、易产生幻觉的LLM模拟器或合成环境，且合成轨迹常过度特化，类似指令序列而非自然人类意图，降低了RL训练效果。

Method: EnvFactory是一个全自动框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 环境构建：自主从真实资源探索并验证状态化、可执行的工具环境；2) 轨迹合成：通过拓扑感知采样和校准细化，生成包含隐式意图的接地查询的自然多轮轨迹。

Result: 仅用85个验证环境（覆盖7个领域），生成2,575条SFT和RL轨迹。相比先前工作使用多达5倍的环境数量，EnvFactory实现了更优的训练效率和下游性能：在BFCLv3上提升Qwen3系列模型最高+15%，MCP-Atlas上+8.6%，对话基准（τ²-Bench和VitaBench）上+6%。

Conclusion: EnvFactory通过全自动化环境和轨迹合成，为Agentic RL提供了可扩展、可扩展且鲁棒的基础，有效克服了现有方法在环境可扩展性和训练数据真实性方面的局限。

Abstract: Equipping LLMs with tool-use capabilities via Agentic Reinforcement Learning (Agentic RL) is bottlenecked by two challenges: the lack of scalable, robust execution environments and the scarcity of realistic training data that captures implicit human reasoning. Existing approaches depend on costly real-world APIs, hallucination-prone LLM simulators, or synthetic environments that are often single-turn or depend on pre-collected documents. Moreover, synthetic trajectories are frequently over-specified, resembling instruction sequences rather than natural human intents, reducing their effectiveness for RL training. We introduce EnvFactory, a fully automated framework that addresses both challenges. EnvFactory autonomously explores and verifies stateful, executable tool environments from authentic resources, and synthesizes natural multi-turn trajectories through topology-aware sampling and calibrated refinement, producing grounded queries with implicit intents. Using only 85 verified environments across 7 domains, EnvFactory generates 2,575 SFT and RL trajectories. Despite using significantly fewer environments than prior work, which are often 5 times more, EnvFactory achieves superior training efficiency and downstream performance, improving Qwen3-series models by up to +15% on BFCLv3, +8.6% on MCP-Atlas, and +6% on conversational benchmarks including $τ^2$-Bench and VitaBench. By fully automating both environment construction and trajectory synthesis, EnvFactory provides a scalable, extensible, and robust foundation for Agentic RL.

</details>


### [204] [Predictable Confabulations: Factual Recall by LLMs Scales with Model Size and Topic Frequency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18732)
*Matthew L. Smith,Jonathan P. Shock,Samuel T. Segun,Iyiola E. Olatunji,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现，大语言模型的事实回忆质量服从一个S型函数，该函数取决于模型参数量和训练数据中主题表示的log线性组合。这两个变量解释了16个密集模型60%的方差，在单个模型家族内可达74-94%。该模式与叠加存储理论一致：回忆由信噪比门控，信号强度随概念频率缩放，噪声基底随模型容量缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律主要描述大语言模型的整体性能，但缺乏将事实回忆与模型规模和训练数据组成联系起来的缩放定律。

Method: 在38个模型上使用自动化参考文献验证系统评估超过8,900条学术参考文献，分析模型参数量和训练数据中主题表示对回忆质量的影响。

Result: 回忆质量遵循模型参数量与训练数据主题表示的log线性组合的S型函数；这两个变量解释16个密集模型60%的方差，单个家族内达74-94%。

Conclusion: 该模式支持叠加存储理论：事实回忆由信噪比（概念频率/模型容量）门控，为理解模型规模与训练数据如何协同影响事实回忆提供了定量框架。

Abstract: While scaling laws govern aggregate large language model performance, no scaling law has linked factual recall to both model size and training-data composition. We evaluated 38 models on over 8,900 scholarly references evaluated by an automated reference verification system. Recall quality follows a sigmoid in the log-linear combination of model parameter count and topic representation in training data. These two variables alone explain 60% of the variance across 16 dense models from four families, rising to 74-94% within individual families. The form matches a superposition-inspired account in which recall is gated by a signal-to-noise ratio: signal strength scales with concept frequency and the noise floor with model capacity.

</details>


### [205] [Code as Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18747)
*Xuying Ning,Katherine Tieu,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Zihao Li,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Mengting Ai,Zhining Liu,Ting-Wei Li,Lingjie Chen,Yanjun Zhao,Ke Yang,Bingxuan Li,Cheng Qian,Gaotang Li,Xiao Lin,Zhichen Zeng,Ruizhong Qiu,Sirui Chen,Yifan Sun,Xiyuan Yang,Ruida Wang,Rui Pan,Chenyuan Yang,Dylan Zhang,Liri Fang,Zikun Cui,Yang Cao,Pan Chen,Dorothy Sun,Ren Chen,Mahesh Srinivasan,Nipun Mathur,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Pan Lu,Lingming Zhang,Tong Zhang,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"代码作为智能体框架"（Code as Agent Harness）的统一视角，将代码视为智能体系统的基础设施，围绕框架接口、框架机制、框架扩展三个层次系统梳理了相关方法与实际应用，并讨论了开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码从仅作为目标输出转变为智能体推理、行动、环境建模和执行验证的核心基础设施，现有研究缺乏对这一转变的系统性梳理和统一视角。

Method: 围绕三个层次组织综述：1）框架接口（推理、行动、环境建模的连接）；2）框架机制（规划、记忆、工具使用、反馈驱动控制与优化）；3）框架扩展（从单智能体到多智能体的共享代码工件与协作）。

Result: 总结了代码辅助编程、GUI/OS自动化、具身智能体、科学发现、个性化推荐、DevOps和企业工作流等领域的代表性方法与实际应用。

Conclusion: 未来挑战包括：超越最终任务成功的评估、不完全反馈下的验证、无回归的框架改进、多智能体间一致共享状态、安全关键行动的人类监督，以及多模态环境扩展。

Abstract: Recent large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in understanding and generating code, from competitive programming to repository-level software engineering. In emerging agentic systems, code is no longer only a target output. It increasingly serves as an operational substrate for agent reasoning, acting, environment modeling, and execution-based verification. We frame this shift through the lens of agent harnesses and introduce code as agent harness: a unified view that centers code as the basis for agent infrastructure. To systematically study this perspective, we organize the survey around three connected layers. First, we study the harness interface, where code connects agents to reasoning, action, and environment modeling. Second, we examine harness mechanisms: planning, memory, and tool use for long-horizon execution, together with feedback-driven control and optimization that make harness reliable and adaptive. Third, we discuss scaling the harness from single-agent systems to multi-agent settings, where shared code artifacts support multi-agent coordination, review, and verification. Across these layers, we summarize representative methods and practical applications of code as agent harness, spanning coding assistants, GUI/OS automation, embodied agents, scientific discovery, personalization and recommendation, DevOps, and enterprise workflows. We further outline open challenges for harness engineering, including evaluation beyond final task success, verification under incomplete feedback, regression-free harness improvement, consistent shared state across multiple agents, human oversight for safety-critical actions, and extensions to multimodal environments. By centering code as the harness of agentic AI, this survey provides a unified roadmap toward executable, verifiable, and stateful AI agent systems.

</details>


### [206] [DashAttention: Differentiable and Adaptive Sparse Hierarchical Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18753)
*Yuxiang Huang,Nuno M. T. Gonçalves,Federico Alvetreti,Lei Li,Xu Han,Edoardo M. Ponti,André F. T. Martins,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DashAttention提出一种可微分且自适应的稀疏层次注意力机制，通过α-entmax变换替代top-k操作，实现可变块数选择并保持全层次可微分，在75%稀疏度下达到全注意力精度，推理速度超越FlashAttention-3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有层次注意力方法（如NSA、InfLLMv2）使用top-k操作选择固定数量的相关KV块，这既假设了任意查询的相关token数量固定，又阻断了稀疏阶段与稠密阶段之间的梯度流，限制了模型的灵活性和可训练性。

Method: 在第一阶段使用自适应稀疏的α-entmax变换替代top-k操作，根据当前查询选择可变数量的块，为第二阶段的softmax注意力提供先验，同时保持整个层次结构的完全可微分性。

Result: DashAttention在75%稀疏度下达到与全注意力相当的精度；相比NSA和InfLLMv2实现了更优的Pareto前沿，尤其在高稀疏度区域；基于Triton的高效实现在推理时比FlashAttention-3更快。

Conclusion: DashAttention提供了一种成本高效的长上下文建模策略，具有非色散特性，有利于更好的长上下文建模能力。

Abstract: Current hierarchical attention methods, such as NSA and InfLLMv2, select the top-k relevant key-value (KV) blocks based on coarse attention scores and subsequently apply fine-grained softmax attention on the selected tokens. However, the top-k operation assumes the number of relevant tokens for any query is fixed and it precludes the gradient flow between the sparse and dense stages. In this work, we propose DashAttention (Differentiable and Adaptive Sparse Hierarchical Attention), which leverages the adaptively sparse $α$-entmax transformation to select a variable number of blocks according to the current query in the first stage. This in turn provides a prior for the second-stage softmax attention, keeping the entire hierarchy fully differentiable. Contrary to other hierarchical attention methods, we show that DashAttention is non-dispersive, translating to better long-context modeling ability. Experiments with large language models (LLMs) show that DashAttention achieves comparable accuracy as full attention with 75% sparsity and a better Pareto frontier than NSA and InfLLMv2, especially in high-sparsity regimes. We also provide an efficient, GPU-aware implementation of DashAttention in Triton, which achieves a speedup of up to over FlashAttention-3 at inference time. Overall, DashAttention offers a cost-effective strategy to model long contexts.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [ANNEAL: Adapting LLM Agents via Governed Symbolic Patch Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16309)
*Safayat Bin Hakim,Keyan Guo,Wenkai Tan,Alvaro Velasquez,Shouhuai Xu,Houbing Herbert Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ANNEAL是一种神经符号智能体，通过故障驱动知识获取（FDKA）机制，将重复性执行失败转化为对过程知识图谱的受控符号编辑，在不修改基础模型权重的情况下实现持久性结构修复，在四个领域中将重复故障率从72-100%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体虽能恢复单次执行错误，但当底层过程知识（操作符模式、前置条件和约束）未被修复时，会在相同故障上反复失败。现有自进化方法通过更新提示、记忆或模型权重来应对，但无法直接修复编码任务执行方式的符号结构，且缺乏安全部署所需的治理保障。

Method: 提出ANNEAL神经符号智能体，核心机制为故障驱动知识获取（FDKA）：定位故障操作符→通过约束LLM生成合成类型化补丁→通过多维评分、符号护栏和金丝雀测试验证提案→提交编辑。每次编辑保留完整溯源和确定性回滚能力。

Result: 在四个领域27次多种子运行中，ANNEAL是唯一实现持久结构修复的系统。强基线（ReAct, Reflexion）虽实现高情节恢复率，但保留72-100%的留出故障率；ANNEAL在测试的重复故障设置中将其降至0%。消融实验表明移除FDKA会消除所有结构修复，成功率下降高达26.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 受控符号修复为持久性故障消除提供了与权重级和提示级适配互补的新范式。

Abstract: LLM-based agents can recover from individual execution errors, yet they repeatedly fail on the same fault when the underlying process knowledge--operator schemas, preconditions, and constraints--remains unrepaired. Existing self-evolving approaches address this gap by updating prompts, memory, or model weights, but none directly repair the symbolic structures that encode how tasks are executed, and few provide the governance guarantees required for safe deployment. We introduce ANNEAL, a neuro-symbolic agent that converts recurring failures into governed symbolic edits of a process knowledge graph without modifying foundation model weights. Its core mechanism, Failure-Driven Knowledge Acquisition (FDKA), localizes the responsible operator, synthesizes a typed patch through constrained LLM generation, and validates the proposal via multi-dimensional scoring, symbolic guardrails, and canary testing before commit. Every accepted edit carries full provenance and deterministic rollback capability. Across four domains and 27 multi-seed runs, ANNEAL is the only evaluated system that commits persistent structural repairs--strong baselines such as ReAct and Reflexion achieve high episodic recovery yet retain 72-100% holdout failure rates on recurring faults, whereas ANNEAL reduces these to 0% in the tested recurring-failure settings. Ablation confirms that removing FDKA eliminates all structural repairs and drops success rate by up to 26.7 percentage points. These results suggest that governed symbolic repair offers a complementary paradigm to weight-level and prompt-level adaptation for persistent fault elimination.

</details>


### [208] [From Prompts to Protocols: An AI Agent for Laboratory Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16552)
*Angelos Angelopoulos,James F. Cahoon,Ron Alterovitz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个集成大语言模型与实验室编排的AI智能体架构，通过自然语言交互实现自动化实验协议的创建、执行与监控，在化学、生物和材料科学三个模拟实验室中达到97%的首轮协议生成成功率，并将所需界面操作减少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化实验室需要协调大量仪器和机器人，科学家不得不编写代码、管理配置文件并应对复杂的软件基础设施，这阻碍了实验自动化的广泛采用。

Method: 将大语言模型集成到实验编排系统（EOS）中，采用带有自动验证和纠错的智能体循环架构，支持完整的实验生命周期（协议创建、运行监控、闭环优化、结果分析），并配有与AI协议表示同步的视觉图形编辑器。

Result: 在三个模拟自动化实验室（化学、生物学、材料科学）的评估中，AI智能体实现了97%的首轮协议生成成功率，并将所需界面操作减少了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该AI智能体架构能有效降低自动化实验系统的使用门槛，让科学家通过自然语言即可交互式创建和监控自动化实验协议，显著提升实验效率。

Abstract: Automating science laboratories enables faster, safer, more accurate, and more reproducible execution of protocols, accelerating the discovery and testing of new materials, drugs, and more. However, setting up and running autonomous labs requires coordinating numerous instruments and robots, forcing scientists to write code, manage configuration files, and navigate complex software infrastructure. We present an AI agent architecture that integrates large language models with laboratory orchestration, enabling scientists to interactively create and monitor automated lab protocols using natural language. Integrated into the Experiment Orchestration System (EOS), the AI agent operates under an agentic loop with automated validation and error correction, and supports the complete experimental lifecycle: creating protocols, running and monitoring both protocols and closed-loop optimization campaigns, and analyzing results. A visual graph editor renders protocols as interactive node-based diagrams synchronized with the AI agent's protocol representation, enabling seamless alternation between AI-assisted and manual protocol construction. Evaluated on three simulated automated labs spanning chemistry, biology, and materials science, the AI agent achieves a 97% first-attempt protocol generation success rate and an order of magnitude reduction in required interface actions.

</details>


### [209] [Scalable Uncertainty Reasoning in Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16568)
*Jingcheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一个模块化框架，通过代数、逻辑和几何三种专门技术，分别处理知识图谱中属性值不精确、三元组存在概率不确定和模式知识不完整这三个层次的不确定性，旨在实现语义精确性与计算可处理性的统一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界数据固有的不确定性在知识图谱中表现为三个层次（属性值不精确、三元组存在概率不确定、模式知识不完整），而当前语义网标准缺乏对这些不确定性的原生推理支持，朴素的扩展方法又往往导致计算不可处理。

Method: (1) 定义概率字面量及对应的连续属性查询代数；(2) 提出基于编译的框架，将SPARQL溯源转换为可处理的概率电路；(3) 采用拓扑感知的几何嵌入进行统计模式推理。

Result: 三个层次的专门推理机制（代数、逻辑、几何）能够分别处理对应层次的不确定性，并在语义精确性和计算可处理性之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 通过代数、逻辑和几何三种专门化的推理机制，可以协调语义精确性与计算可处理性，实现对知识图谱中多层次不确定性的有效推理。

Abstract: Knowledge Graphs are pivotal for semantic data integration. The real-world data they model is often inherently uncertain. Within knowledge graphs, uncertainty manifests in three distinct levels: imprecise attribute values, probabilistic triple existence, and incomplete schema knowledge. However, current Semantic Web standards lack native support for reasoning over such uncertainty, and naïve extensions often incur computational intractability. In this thesis, I aim to develop a modular framework that addresses each level through tailored techniques: (1) defining probabilistic literals and a corresponding query algebra for continuous attributes; (2) a compilation-based framework transforming SPARQL provenance into tractable probabilistic circuits for uncertain triples; and (3) topology-aware geometric embeddings for statistical schema reasoning. The central hypothesis is that specialized reasoning mechanisms, namely algebraic, logical, and geometric approaches, can reconcile semantic precision with computational tractability.

</details>


### [210] [TTE-Flash: Accelerating Reasoning-based Multimodal Representations via Think-Then-Embed Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16638)
*Jianpeng Cheng,Xian Wu,Jiangfan Zhang,Wentao Bao,Chaitanya Ahuja,Shlok Kumar Mishra,Hanchao Yu,Yang Gao,Fan Xia,Qi Guo,Shaodan Zhai,Xiangjun Fan,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用隐式思考令牌（latent think tokens）替代显式思维链（CoT）推理，在保持多模态嵌入性能的同时将推理成本降至常数，并引入TTE-Flash-2B模型在MMEB-v2基准上超越显式CoT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式CoT推理虽然能提升通用多模态嵌入（UME）的性能，但其生成显式推理轨迹的计算开销过高，亟需一种既能保持推理感知能力又能降低计算成本的方法。

Method: 将显式CoT替换为隐式思考令牌（视为潜在变量），思考令牌通过CoT生成损失优化，后续的嵌入令牌通过对比损失优化，实现推理感知表示；同时研究了从同一LLM骨干提取思考令牌与嵌入令牌的架构设计，以及两者作为依赖任务的训练方式。

Result: 提出的TTE-Flash-2B在MMEB-v2基准上超越显式CoT版本；在15个视频数据集上的零样本评估显示，增加思考令牌数量表现出缩放行为，并启发了基于任务需求的自适应思考预算分配。

Conclusion: 隐式思考令牌能有效替代显式CoT，在保持推理能力的同时实现恒定推理成本，且生成的思考令牌在文本和视觉上均可解释，为高效推理感知多模态表示提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent research has demonstrated that Universal Multimodal Embedding (UME) benefits significantly from Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. In this paradigm, a generative model produces explicit reasoning traces for a multimodal query, with the final representation extracted from an <eos> embedding token attending to both the query and the reasoning. Despite its effectiveness, the computational overhead of generating explicit CoT traces is often prohibitive. In this work, we propose replacing explicit CoT with latent think tokens, which are interpreted as latent variables that can produce explicit CoT traces as observed variables. By optimizing think tokens using CoT generation loss and subsequent embedding tokens using contrastive loss, we produce high-performance, reasoning-aware representations at a constant inference cost. Our study investigates two key architectural designs: 1) how think and embeddings tokens should be extracted from the same LLM backbone. 2) how the tokens should be trained as two dependent tasks. We introduce TTE-Flash-2B, a reasoning-aware multimodal representation model that outperforms its explicit-CoT counterpart on the MMEB-v2 benchmark, while producing latent think tokens that are interpretable both textually and visually. Furthermore, zero-shot evaluation across 15 video datasets reveals scaling behavior as the number of think tokens increases, and motivating a pilot study of adaptive think budget allocation based on task requirements.

</details>


### [211] [LinAlg-Bench: A Forensic Benchmark Revealing Structural Failure Modes in LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16675)
*Shradha Agarwal,Deepak Rajbhar,Tariq J*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LinAlg-Bench是一个诊断性基准，用于评估10个前沿大语言模型在3x3、4x4和5x5矩阵上的结构化线性代数计算能力。通过660个SymPy认证的问题和9种任务类型，评估了6,600个模型输出。引入三阶段自动化取证流程，对1,156个失败案例进行十类错误标签分类。核心发现是4x4尺度存在行为阈值：低于该阈值时模型因执行错误失败，高于阈值则转向计算放弃，表现为工具角色扮演、约束一致混淆和结构化幻觉。这种从编造到放弃的转变在所有模型层级中普遍存在，表明是工作记忆限制而非知识差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数学推理评估多关注最终答案正确性，缺乏对失败模式的细粒度分析。线性代数作为结构化数学推理的典型领域，其矩阵维度梯度变化能揭示模型推理能力的边界与失效机制。

Method: 构建LinAlg-Bench基准，包含3x3、4x4、5x5三个维度梯度的9种任务类型共660个SymPy验证问题；评估10个前沿LLM；设计三阶段自动化取证流程（错误检测、分类、细粒度标注），将1,156个失败案例归入10个主要错误标签及子类型。

Result: 发现4x4尺度行为阈值：低维时失败主因是执行错误（符号追踪失败、算术漂移、奇偶错误），高维时失败转为计算放弃（工具角色扮演、约束一致混淆、结构化幻觉）；三种尺度涌现性错误类型仅在4x4和5x5出现；解题策略僵化度是5x5行列式准确率的近完美预测指标；发现约束感知混淆作为新型结构化幻觉故障模式。

Conclusion: LLM数学失败并非随机，而是由算法类型和矩阵维度结构化约束；工作记忆限制而非知识差距是主要瓶颈；现有模型在结构化数学推理中存在系统性局限，需要新的架构设计来突破计算伸缩性瓶颈。

Abstract: We introduce LinAlg-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark evaluating 10 frontier large language models on structured linear algebra computation across a strict dimensional gradient of 3x3, 4x4, and 5x5 matrices. Spanning 9 task types and 660 SymPy-certified problems, the benchmark exhaustively evaluates 6,600 model outputs. Beyond binary accuracy, LinAlg-Bench introduces a three-stage automated forensic pipeline classifying 1,156 failures into ten primary error tags with fine-grained subtypes, revealing that LLM mathematical failure is not random but structurally constrained by algorithm type and matrix dimension. Our central finding is a sharp behavioral threshold at 4x4 scale: below it, models fail through execution errors -- sign tracking failures, arithmetic drift, and parity errors; above it, failure transitions to computational abandonment, with models fabricating responses through tool roleplay, constraint-consistent confabulation, and structured hallucination rather than attempting computation. This fabrication-to-abandonment transition is near-universal across all model tiers and architectures, suggesting a working memory limit rather than a knowledge gap, supported by three scale-emergent error types absent at 3x3 but present at 4x4 and 5x5. We further show that solution strategy rigidity is a near-perfect predictor of 5x5 determinant accuracy, document constraint-aware confabulation as a novel structured hallucination failure mode, and release all data, model outputs, error labels, and judge pipeline publicly.

</details>


### [212] [Recall Isn't Enough: Bounding Commitments in Personalized Language Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16712)
*Rui Tang,Yichi Zhang,Xi Chen,Chen Dong,Youwei Yang,Yumeng Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为CBEA+LCV的方法，通过有界证据激活和词典式承诺验证，在长上下文与记忆系统中实现了零失败操作，尽管召回率较低但输入负载降低了74-75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文和记忆系统将个性化视为召回问题，但在实际执行中常因不恰当的承诺（如将噪声暗示转为硬约束、丢弃罕见证据、遗忘下游义务或回答不可行问题）导致失败。

Method: CBEA：使用类型化覆盖、尾随证据和后果债务激活有界证据集；LCV：在生成文本前验证结构化承诺，将不可行状态路由到修复、弃权或重新契约。

Result: 在360个测试用例和三个生成后端上，CBEA+LCV在验证器范围内实现零失败，可用性为0.49-0.60；原始和长上下文基线使用相同LCV门控仅达到0.003-0.092可用性。CBEA+LCV召回0.012的未编译可见事实，而原始召回0.53。

Conclusion: CBEA+LCV提供了一个有界操作点：显式的承诺控制和降低74-75%的中位输入负载，而非通用记忆优势。

Abstract: Long-context and memory systems usually treat personalization as a recall problem. In practice, many failures occur later, when a system commits: it turns noisy hints into hard constraints, drops rare witnesses, forgets downstream obligations, or answers despite infeasibility. We introduce Contract-Bounded Evidence Activation (CBEA) with Lexicographic Commitment Validation (LCV). CBEA activates a bounded evidence set using typed coverage, tail witnesses, and consequence debt; LCV validates structured commitments before prose and routes infeasible states to repair, abstention, or recontract. Across 360 fixtures and three generation backends, CBEA+LCV reaches zero failures within validator scope at 0.49-0.60 availability over attempted runs. Raw and long-context baselines with the same LCV gate reach zero only at 0.003-0.092. A shadow oracle diagnostic marks the limit: CBEA+LCV recalls 0.012 of uncompiled visible facts, while raw recalls 0.53. The result is a bounded operating point: explicit commitment control and 74-75% lower median input payload, not universal memory dominance.

</details>


### [213] [Baba in Wonderland: Online Self-Supervised Dynamics Discovery for Executable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16725)
*SeungWon Seo,DongHeun Han,SeongRae Noh,HyeongYeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Alice系统，在语义标签不可靠（先验错配）环境下，通过将失败候选更新作为结构信号，利用冲突保留和分类感知探索，实现在线可执行世界模型的学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可执行世界模型依赖语义标签或规则描述，无法在标签与真实动态无关（先验错配）的环境下有效学习，需要一种仅从交互证据中归纳状态依赖动态的方法。

Method: Alice采用闭环系统：当候选更新解释新转换但丢失已解释转换时，将保留冲突作为结构性信号；通过冲突精炼为假设类，提供分层保留反例用于更新，并引导前沿探索朝向当前程序未充分覆盖的新转换。

Result: 在Baba in Wonderland（先验错配版Baba Is You）实验中，Alice显著提升了先验错配下的可执行世界模型学习性能；消融实验表明分类精炼和分类感知探索均有贡献。

Conclusion: 失败候选更新中的保留冲突可作为有价值的结构信号，通过分类精炼和分类感知探索可有效应对先验错配问题，为在无可靠语义先验环境下学习可执行世界模型提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Executable world models can be read, edited, executed, and reused for planning, but only if the program captures the environment's transition law rather than semantic shortcuts in its surface vocabulary. We study online executable world-model learning under prior misalignment, where an agent must induce state-dependent dynamics from interaction evidence alone, without rule descriptions, reward signals, or trustworthy lexical priors. We introduce Alice, a closed-loop system that treats failed candidate updates as structural signal: when a candidate explains a new transition but loses previously explained ones, the preservation conflict reveals dynamics that the current program had conflated. Alice refines these conflicts into hypothesis classes that both provide compact, class-stratified preservation counterexamples for update and guide frontier exploration toward transitions that are novel and underrepresented with respect to the current program. We evaluate Alice on Baba in Wonderland, a prior-misaligned variant of Baba Is You that preserves simulator dynamics while replacing semantically meaningful rule-property labels with unrelated words. Experiments show that Alice substantially improves executable world-model learning under prior misalignment, and ablations show that both class refinement and class-aware exploration contribute.

</details>


### [214] [Body-Grounded Perspective Formation and Conative Attunement in Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16728)
*Hongju Pae*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于人工智能体身体锚定视角形成的最小架构，通过内感受生存信号、Fisher度量融合内外感受状态以及意动对齐机制，在无奖励的网格世界中实现稳定的身体导向行为，并展示了身体扰动如何在视角潜变量中留下可恢复的几何痕迹，从而在现象学意义上操作化人工主体性的最小结构条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对身体锚定视角形成的最小结构条件的系统研究，尤其是在无需外部奖励的条件下，如何通过身体组织实现主体性体验的现象学可操作化。

Method: 提出一个包含三部分的最小架构：(1) 内感受生存信号（interoceptive viability signal）；(2) 基于Fisher度量的外感受-内感受融合状态度量；(3) 将身体倾向（bodily tendency）转化为行动准备（action readiness）的意动对齐机制（conative alignment）。在无奖励的网格世界中进行实验验证。

Result: 意动机制能将习得的身体倾向转化为稳定的身体导向行为；身体到视角的路由（body-to-perspective routing）使得身体扰动可以在视角潜变量中留下可恢复的几何痕迹。

Conclusion: 研究表明，人工主体性在现象学意义上的最小结构条件可以通过身体组织的具体方式（即世界如何被给予智能体）来操作化实现。

Abstract: This paper proposes a minimal architecture for body-grounded perspective formation in artificial agents. Extending prior work, the model introduces an interoceptive viability signal, a Fisher-style metric over fused exteroceptive-interoceptive states, and a conative alignment mechanism linking bodily tendency to action readiness. In a reward-free gridworld, conation converts learned bodily tendency into stable body-directed behavior, while body-to-perspective routing allows bodily perturbations to leave a recoverable geometric residue in the perspective latent. This study shows how minimal structural conditions for artificial subjectivity can be operationalized in the phenomenological sense, through the embodied organization of how a world is given to an agent.

</details>


### [215] [State Contamination in Memory-Augmented LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16746)
*Yian Wang,Agam Goyal,Yuen Chen,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM智能体中的"记忆清洗"现象：有毒或对抗性上下文被压缩成记忆摘要后，可规避毒性检测但仍能影响下游生成。作者提出次阈值传播差（SPG）量化该问题，发现干预时机至关重要——在摘要化前消毒效果显著，而事后清理可能无效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的安全性不仅取决于单次输出，还依赖于其存储和复用的持久状态（如转录、摘要、检索上下文、记忆缓冲区）。当前缺乏对毒性信息通过记忆压缩规避检测这一失败模式的研究。

Method: 使用配对反事实多智能体展开实验，使得毒性来源的记忆摘要与匹配的中性基线进行对比。提出次阈值传播差（SPG）指标，量化在监控器判定为安全的记忆状态下下游行为的差异。

Result: 1) 毒性来源的记忆摘要可保持在常见毒性阈值以下，但下游毒性仍高于中性基线；2) 原始转录重用驱动显式下游毒性，而压缩记忆携带隐藏的阈值下影响；3) 在摘要化前对毒性状态消毒可显著降低隐藏传播差，而仅清理已完成的摘要可能无法消除清洗后的影响。

Conclusion: 记忆增强型智能体的安全应被视为一个对演化上下文进行状态控制的问题，消毒应在不安全信息被压缩进持久记忆之前应用。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on persistent state, including transcripts, summaries, retrieved context, and memory buffers, to support long-horizon interaction. This makes safety depend not only on individual model outputs, but also on what an agent stores and later reuses. We study a failure mode we call memory laundering: toxic or adversarial context can be compressed into memory summaries that no longer appear toxic under standard detectors, while still preserving hostile framing or conflict structure that influences future generations. Using paired counterfactual multi-agent rollouts, we show that toxic-origin memory summaries can remain below common toxicity thresholds while nevertheless increasing downstream toxicity relative to matched neutral baselines. To measure this hidden influence, we introduce the sub-threshold propagation gap (SPG), which quantifies downstream behavioral differences conditioned on memory states that a deployed monitor would classify as safe. Our experiments show that toxicity propagates through distinct state channels: raw transcript reuse drives overt downstream toxicity, while compressed memory carries hidden sub-threshold influence. We further find that mitigation depends critically on intervention placement. Sanitizing toxic state before summarization substantially reduces the hidden propagation gap, whereas cleaning only the completed summary can leave laundered influence intact. These results suggest that safety in memory-augmented agents should be treated as a state-control problem over evolving context, with sanitization applied before unsafe information is compressed into persistent memory.

</details>


### [216] [Multi-Paradigm Agent Interaction in Practice:A Systematic Analysis of Generator-Evaluator, ReAct Loop,and Adversarial Evaluation in the buddyMe Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16821)
*Xiaohua Wang,Chao Han,Kai Yu,XiaoLiang Xu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了 buddyMe 框架中三种智能体交互范式（多智能体编排、ReAct工具循环、记忆增强交互），通过五阶段处理流水线和六维评估体系，基于四个真实案例得出关键结论：生成器-评估器可检测20%复杂任务的遗漏需求，ReAct循环存在30%冗余工具调用，对抗性讨论在70%场景中2-3轮内达成共识。研究为构建稳定可靠的多范式智能体系统提供了设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体产生了多种交互范式，但很少有生产系统能在统一架构内集成多种范式。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过 buddyMe 框架系统分析三种主要交互范式，并提供实际设计指南。

Method: 提出五阶段处理流水线（需求预审→任务分解→ReAct执行→实执行验证→对抗性评估讨论）和六维加权的评估模式。通过四个真实部署案例（博物馆导览生成、定时天气任务、综合旅游规划）进行实证研究，并使用Mermaid图表进行架构可视化及跨范式系统对比。

Result: 1) 生成器-评估器预审检测出20%复杂任务的遗漏需求，80%任务通过初始检查；2) ReAct循环确保子任务稳定执行但产生约30%冗余工具调用；3) 70%场景下对抗性评估-防御者讨论在2-3轮内达成共识，主要进行内容精炼而非逻辑反转。

Conclusion: 研究结果为构建稳定可靠的多范式智能体系统提供了实用设计指南，指出了各范式的优势与局限性（如ReAct的冗余调用问题），并通过跨系统对比验证了框架的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of Large Language Model (LLM) agents has produced diverse interaction paradigms, yet few production systems integrate multiple paradigms within a unified architecture. This paper presents a systematic analysis of three principal agent interaction paradigms, including Multi-Agent Orchestration (Generator-Evaluator), ReAct Tool-Use Loops, and Memory-Augmented Interaction, as implemented in buddyMe, an open-source multi-model agent programming framework. We formalize a five-stage processing pipeline: Requirement Pre-Review -> Task Decomposition -> ReAct Execution -> Real-Execution Verification -> Adversarial Evaluation Discussion, and establish a six-dimensional evaluation schema with weighted scoring. Through four empirical case studies drawn from real-world deployment logs covering museum guide generation, scheduled weather tasks, and comprehensive tour planning, we draw three key conclusions. First, Generator-Evaluator pre-review detects requirement omissions in 20 percent of complex tasks, with 80 percent tasks passing initial inspection. Second, the ReAct loop ensures stable subtask execution but leads to around 30 percent redundant tool invocations. Third, adversarial Evaluator-Defender discussions reach consensus within 2-3 rounds for nearly 70 percent of scenarios, functioning mainly for content refinement rather than logical reversal. We additionally provide three Mermaid-based architectural diagrams and conduct cross-paradigm comparisons with CrewAI, AutoGen, LangGraph, MemGPT and A-Mem across six system dimensions. The research outcomes offer practical design guidelines for constructing stable and reliable multi-paradigm agent systems.

</details>


### [217] [Voices in the Loop: Mapping Participatory AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16827)
*Rashid Mushkani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个参与式AI倡议的开放存储库和交互式图谱，基于可重复协议对全球倡议进行地理编码、分层和生命周期分析，发现参与式AI集中在少数国家且多发生在问题制定、评估和治理阶段而非模型开发阶段，并提出了一个“默认参与式”AI基础设施的设计与治理框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与式AI方法在公共、公民和人道主义领域日益增多，但关于参与如何组织的证据仍然碎片化，缺乏系统性的比较研究和标准化协议。

Method: 基于Magaña和Shilton的Trustworthy AI语料库及其他审计案例，制定了可重复的发现、审核、协调、地理编码、溯源追踪和发布协议，构建了开放存储库和交互式图谱。

Result: 参与式AI倡议集中在少数国家；参与最常见于问题制定、评估和治理阶段，而非模型开发或训练阶段；图谱通过版本化发布、链接问题与注释渠道、模式反馈工作流及编辑/受限披露请求实现了“默认参与式”AI基础设施的设计与治理框架。

Conclusion: 该图谱作为一个可更新、可争论、可复用的活目录，支持比较研究、政策学习和社区审查。

Abstract: Participatory approaches to artificial intelligence are increasingly documented across public, civic, and humanitarian settings, but evidence about how participation is organized remains fragmented. This paper reports on the construction of an open repository and interactive atlas of participatory AI initiatives, using records harmonized from Maga~na and Shilton's Trustworthy AI corpus, and additional audited cases from research and practice. We contribute three elements. First, we specify a reproducible protocol for discovery, vetting, harmonization, geocoding, provenance tracking, and release-based publication of participatory AI records. Second, we report corpus-level patterns in geography, participation tiers, lifecycle loci, organizational form, verification status, and remaining documentation gaps. Documented initiatives remain concentrated in a small number of countries, while participation is most often coded at problem formulation, evaluation, and governance rather than model development or training. Third, we show how the atlas operationalizes a design and governance framework for participatory-by-default AI infrastructures through versioned releases, record-linked issue and annotation channels, schema feedback workflows, and redaction or restricted-disclosure requests. The atlas is intended to support comparative research, policy learning, and community scrutiny through a living inventory that can be updated, contested, and reused.

</details>


### [218] [Artificial Adaptive Intelligence: The Missing Stage Between Narrow and General Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16844)
*Boris Kriuk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并定义了一个名为"人工自适应智能(AAI)"的新范式，指代介于狭义人工智能与通用人工智能之间的领域，其核心原则是将人类从参数指定中逐步移除，通过数据驱动的超参数自适应实现系统的参数最小化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习研究中，狭义AI系统与通用AI之间存在着一个未被命名的研究领域，该领域涵盖元学习、神经架构搜索、AutoML、持续学习、进化计算和物理信息建模等方向，它们都隐含着同一个原则——逐步减少人类在参数指定中的参与，但缺乏统一的理论框架和概念命名。

Method: (1) 定义了AAI的操作性定义：系统无需人工指定的可调超参数，且在多样任务分布上保持竞争性能；(2) 提出了量化AAI程度的适应指数，该指数将系统吸收的超参数比例与对任务特化基线的性能比值相结合；(3) 基于最小描述长度原则建立了参数最小化的理论框架；(4) 将领域组织为三条实现路径：数据和任务感知配置、结构与进化形态变换、以及训练内自适应。

Result: 通过航空航天设计、金融体制检测、湍流建模、生态动力学和视觉语言系统等多个案例研究，展示了AAI原则在不同领域的适用性；提出了适应指数作为量化进度度量；建立了参数最小化的理论保障。

Conclusion: 从狭义人工智能通往通用人工智能的路径必然经过人工自适应智能，命名这一阶段将改变我们衡量什么、构建什么以及如何定义成功。

Abstract: Between the narrow systems we deploy and the general intelligence we speculate about lies an entire regime of machine behavior that has never received its own name. This monograph argues that this regime is not empty: it is where meta-learning, neural architecture search, AutoML, continual learning, evolutionary computation, and physics-informed modeling have quietly converged on a common principle, namely the steady removal of the human from the loop of parameter specification. We name this regime Artificial Adaptive Intelligence (AAI) and define it operationally: a system exhibits AAI to the extent that it requires no human-specified tunable hyperparameters while maintaining competitive performance across a diverse distribution of tasks. To make the definition quantitative, we introduce an adaptivity index that measures progress along an axis orthogonal to scale, combining the fraction of hyperparameters absorbed by the system with the performance ratio against a task-specialized baseline. We develop the principle of parametric minimality and ground it in the minimum description length framework, showing that the appropriate hyperparameter count is data-determined rather than designer-determined. We then organize the field around three pathways to minimality: data- and task-aware configuration, structural and evolutionary morphing, and in-training self-adaptation. We analyze their stability, convergence, and governance implications, and illustrate them through case studies spanning aerospace design, financial regime detection, turbulence modeling, ecological dynamics, and vision-language systems. The thesis is that the path from ANI to AGI passes through AAI, and that naming this stage changes what we measure, what we build, and what we call a success.

</details>


### [219] [Learning to Learn from Multimodal Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16857)
*Xingyu Sui,Weixiang Zhao,Yongxin Tang,Yanyan Zhao,Yang Wu,Dandan Tu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种新的"从多模态经验中学习"范式，将记忆设计从预定义组件转变为自适应可学习过程，使智能体能够根据任务需求和交互历史动态构建、组织和利用记忆，在多模态任务中显著提升性能与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验驱动学习方法主要在文本环境中开发，依赖人工设计的记忆模式，无法有效处理真实世界中感知、推理和行动等多模态异构信号，且最优记忆结构高度依赖于具体任务并随时间演化，固定记忆设计存在根本局限。
